# Mangrove Gem
Aliases: Groovy
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Craftable items, Mangrove Log Collection
[Mangrove Gem]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Mangrove Log V (5) ([[Mangrove Log]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Groovy
Applied To: [[Armor]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXX (30) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 23,040x (360 stacks (23,040 items total)) [[Mangrove Log]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MANGROVE_GEM
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Groovy reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Foraging Fortune; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; RARE REFORGE STONE
A Mangrove Gem is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]]. It can be used to apply the [[Reforging|Groovy]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to armor. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Log V (5)]]. It grants [[Foraging Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mangrove Log V (5) ([[Mangrove Log]])|144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] x16 ×9 → Mangrove Gem|
## Usage
### Reforging
Mangrove Gem can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Groovy]] reforge, to grant [[Foraging Fortune]], which scales with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Groovy]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+3 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+4 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+5 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+6 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+7 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+8 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|

The Mantis Shrimp Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Great Treasure]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Mantis Shrimp Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Mantis Shrimp]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Mantis Shrimp Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Mantis Shrimp Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Great Treasure]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Great Treasure ([[Attributes]]); [[Treasure]] catches are +1%–10% more likely to be GREAT.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Mantis Shrimp Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Marsh Spore Soup
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Marsh Spore Soup]
Type: Consumable item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: A bubbling soup filled with poisonous mushrooms.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MARSH_SPORE_SOUP
Tooltip: A bubbling soup filled with poisonous mushrooms.; Right-click to consume!; RARE
Marsh Spore Soup is a [[Rarity|RARE]] consumable item.
## Obtaining
Marsh Spore Soup can be obtained from [[Ezekiel]] for 5 [[Mushroom Spore]] and 1,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
When consumed, Marsh Spore Soup surrounds the player with Nausea effect particles and [[Mushroom|Brown Mushrooms]] will randomly explode around them. The effects will last for 80 seconds.

A Matcho Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Matcho]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Matcho Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Ignition]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Ignition ([[Attributes]]); Increases the duration of fire damage that you inflict on enemies by +30%–300%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Matcho Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Matcho Shard has the following description:
- Matchos are quick to ignite, their volatile nature making them dangerous to approach.
## Trivia
- Despite being a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Matcho Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

A Medium Fishing Net is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Sea Lumies|Sea Lumies V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting

## Usage
Fishing Net:
Medium Fishing Net [[Pull]]: +25
[Effects]
[Ability: Yoink RIGHT CLICK; Throw your fishing net at swimming fish to catch them!; Throw Distance: +50%; Cooldown: 0.5s Ability: Grab; Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Water Shards (Attributes) caught with this net.]
A Medium Fishing Net can be used to catch [[Attributes|Water Attribute Shards]]. Medium Fishing Net or better is required to catch [[Flipflopper|Flipfloppers]] and [[Seashine|Seashines]].
The throw distance of Medium Fishing Nets is 12 blocks.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MEDIUM_POCKET_BLACK_HOLE
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Black Hole RIGHT CLICK Place the Black Hole for 180s, and it'll start pulling low HP monsters towards it.; Cooldown: 2s; Harness the power of Gravity!; Monsters below 10% HP can even get their Shard captured!; Ability: Blessing from the Dark Grants 2.5 Hunting Fortune on Combat Shards caught with this Black Hole!; RARE DEPLOYABLE
A Medium Pocket Black Hole is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Deployables|Deployable]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Medium Pocket Black Hole can be purchased from [[Albert]].
|Name|Ingredients|
|-|-|
|Medium Pocket Black Hole|64x [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] 64x [[Enchanted Obsidian]] 1x [[Small Pocket Black Hole]] 1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
## Usage
A Medium Pocket Black Hole can be placed to pull in mobs under 10% [[Health]] to capture their [[Attributes|Shard]]. Additionally, its Blessing from the Dark ability grants +2.5 [[Hunting Fortune]] on Combat Shards.
The deployable lasts for 180s, and resets its duration every time a mob is captured.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Large Pocket Black Hole]]|Ruby Veilshroom VIII (8) ([[Ruby Veilshroom]])|[[Medium Pocket Black Hole]] 1x Medium Pocket Black Hole 2x [[Veilshroom Bunch]] 5x [[Helixis]] 8x [[Bottled Mischief]]|Recipe: [[Veilshroom Bunch]] ×2 + [[Bottled Mischief]] x8 + [[Helixis]] ×5 + Medium Pocket Black Hole → [[Large Pocket Black Hole]]|
## Bugs
- Using a Medium Pocket Black Hole on certain mobs may void [[Bestiary]] credit and/or regular item loot.
## Trivia
- Contrary to its name, the Medium Pocket Black Hole only has a Schwarzschild radius of 47.1 zeptometers (4.71 × 10⁻²⁰ meters).

# Melon Juice Mixin

[Melon Juice Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 8,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 16x [[Melon Juice]] 2x [[Soggybud]] 1x [[Glass Bottle]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MELON_JUICE_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Grants +15 Farming Fortune; Duration: 36h +36h Default The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; RARE
A Melon Juice Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] 2x [[Soggybud]] 16x [[Melon Juice]]|Recipe: [[Melon Juice]] x16 + [[Soggybud]] x2 + [[Glass Bottle]] → Melon Juice Mixin|
## Usage
### Brewing
A Melon Juice Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player the following stats:
- +15 [[Farming Fortune]]
This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Melon Juice Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.

The Mercenary Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor]] set that can be bought from [[Rosetta]]. It is meant to be used with a [[Mercenary Sword]].
## Obtaining
The Mercenary Armor can be purchased from [[Rosetta]] for a total of 180,000 [[Coins]] after the player has reached [[Combat|Combat IX (9)]].
Cost to purchase:
|Slot|Cost|
|-|-|
|Mercenary Helmet|35,000 [[Coins]]|
|Mercenary Chestplate|70,000 [[Coins]]|
|Mercenary Leggings|45,000 [[Coins]]|
|Mercenary Boots|30,000 [[Coins]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Mercenary Helmet ([[Mercenary Armor]]); [[Health]]: +40 [[Defense]]: +40 [[Strength]]: +10|Mercenary Chestplate ([[Mercenary Armor]]); [[Health]]: +70 [[Defense]]: +70 [[Strength]]: +10|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Mercenary Leggings ([[Mercenary Armor]]); [[Health]]: +60 [[Defense]]: +60 [[Strength]]: +10|Mercenary Boots ([[Mercenary Armor]]); [[Health]]: +30 [[Defense]]: +30 [[Strength]]: +10|
[Total]
[Mercenary Armor Health: +200 Defense: +200 Strength: +40]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Death Tax; Earn 5 Coins and heal 20 Health each kill.]
The Mercenary Armor requires [[Combat|Combat IX (9)]] to use.
When the full set is worn, every enemy killed which is level 10 or higher grants the wearer 5 [[Coins]] and heals them for 20 [[Health]].
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Knight Skin]]|[[Knight Skin (Mercenary)]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Event Shop]] - Summer 2025 - 100 Event Silver|
## Trivia
- It is the first and currently only armor which increases [[Coins|Coin]] gain.
- When combined with [[Daedalus Blade]] and level 100 [[Hound Pet]] player can get additional 45 [[Coins]] per kill.
- This armor requires 36,000 kills to break even with its cost, so it's only recommended to buy it if you're very early in the game and plan on grinding large amounts of mobs.
- The Mercenary Armor shares the Color ⁠ #E0FCF7 with [[Speedster Armor]].
## History

|Jul 15, 2026|Added +10 [[Strength]] to each piece.|
|Jul 15, 2026|Added a 0.5s cooldown.|

|Sep 7, 2021|Added Mercenary Armor.|

|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Added a 0.5s cooldown.|

# Meteor Chunk
Aliases: Fortified, Meteor Shard
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Craftable items, Ghast Tear Collection
[Meteor Chunk]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Ghast Tear VI (6) ([[Ghast Tear]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Fortified
Applied To: [[Equipment]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXIV (24) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 41,150 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 5,000x (78 stacks plus 8 (5,000 in total)) [[Ghast Tear]] 20x [[Starfall]] 10x [[Tentacle Meat]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: METEOR_SHARD
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Fortified reforge when combined with Equipment.; A very strange find once found inside the carcass of a Ghast. It seems like they enjoy a snack that is out of this world from time to time.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Defense; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Meteor Chunk is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]], which can apply the [[Reforging|Fortified]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Ghast Tear|Ghast Tear VI (6)]]. It grants [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ghast Tear VI (6) ([[Ghast Tear]])|10x [[Tentacle Meat]] 20x [[Starfall]] 40x [[Silver Fang]]|Recipe: [[Silver Fang]] x10 ×4 + [[Starfall]] x5 ×4 + [[Tentacle Meat]] x10 → Meteor Chunk|
## Usage
### Reforging
Meteor Chunk can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Fortified]] reforge, to grant [[Defense]], which scales with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Fortified]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+12 Def ([[Defense]])|+14 Def ([[Defense]])|+17 Def ([[Defense]])|+20 Def ([[Defense]])|+25 Def ([[Defense]])|+30 Def ([[Defense]])|+35 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|

Millennia-Old Blaze Ashes are a [[Rarity|RARE]] item dropped by [[Millennia-Aged Blaze|Millennia-Aged Blazes]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops

A Mimic Fragment is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeons]] item that is obtained by killing a [[Mimic]], which can appear on [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor 6]] and above.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops

## Trivia
- The Mimic Fragment's lore, "Fool me once, shame on you. Fool me 9 times, and you can craft a [[Lesser Orb of Healing]]" is a reference to a saying by Confucius: "Fool me once, shame on you, fool me twice, shame on me".

# Minion Expander
Aliases: Expander
Categories: Rare items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Nether Quartz Collection, Minion Modifiers
[Minion Expander]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Nether Quartz V (5) ([[Nether Quartz]])
Function: Increases the minion range by one block and increases minion speed by 5%.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 4,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2,560x (40 stacks (2,560 items total)) [[Nether Quartz]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MINION_EXPANDER
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. Increases the effective radius of the minion by 1 extra block.; Increases the speed of your minion by 5%.; RARE
The Minion Expander is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion upgrade]] that increases minion range by +1 block and minion speed by 5%. It is unlocked at [[Nether Quartz|Nether Quartz V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Nether Quartz V (5) ([[Nether Quartz]])|2x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 16x [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] x2 ×8 + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x2 → Minion Expander|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Minion Expander can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It increases minion range by +1 block and minion speed by 5% per applied Minion Expander.
|Amount of; Minion Expanders|Usable Area|Speed Boost|
|-|-|-|
|0|5x5|+0%|
|1|7x7|+5%|
|2|9x9|+10%|
Although these items increase the area the minion uses, additional space is not required for the speed boost.
## Tips
- With increased minion range, mob-spawning minions might spawn mobs in a way where they can escape the minion's radius, unless their surrounding is changed after equipping the minions with the item.
## Bugs
- When a Minion Expander is taken out of a mob spawning minion, the expanded spawn range persists until the island is unloaded. The kill range and speed boost do change correctly. The freed up upgrade slot can be used for a different upgrade.
## Trivia
- The Minion Expander's sprite is one of a Command Block, but it cannot be placed or edited.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Minion Expander.|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Now works with Foraging Minions.|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Makes minions go 5% faster.|

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: MINOTAUR
Tooltip: Mythological Fortune I Grants +0.5  Magic Find against  Mythological mobs.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE COMBAT SHARD (ID R63)
The Minotaur Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Mythological Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Minotaur Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Minotaur]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Minotaur Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Minotaur Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mythological Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mythological Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] against [[Mythological]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Minotaur Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Minotaur Shard has the following description:
- Having been mistreated its entire life, the Minotaur opts to lash out preemptively rather than risk being hurt again.

# Miria's Prize
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items
[Miria's Prize]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MIRIA_PRIZE
Tooltip: Used to purchase certain items from Miria's Shop!; EPIC
A Miria's Prize is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Miria's Prize has a 10% chance of being awarded from an [[Rarity|EPIC]] or a 20% chance from a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher bracket in a [[Starlyn Contest]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Usage
Miria's Prizes are used to purchase various [[Foraging]] related items or bonuses from [[Miria]].
### NPC Trading
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|
|[[Helix Fortune]] 41 - 50|Miria's Milestone I ([[Miria]])|[[Miria's Prize]] 1 Miria's Prize per level|
|First [[Foraging]] Level Cap|Miria's Milestone IV ([[Miria]])|[[Miria's Prize]] 2 Miria's Prize|
|Second [[Foraging]] Level Cap|Miria's Milestone V ([[Miria]])|[[Miria's Prize]] 5 Miria's Prize|
|[[Vial of Spring Water]]|Miria's Milestone V ([[Miria]])|[[Miria's Prize]] 10 Miria's Prize|
## Trivia
- A total of 37 Miria's Prizes are needed to buy everything from [[Miria]]. 7 for [[Foraging]] Level Caps, 10 for [[Helix Fortune]] upgrades, and 20 for [[Vial of Spring Water]].

Tooltip: Breaking Power 5; Damage: +65; Mining Speed: +450; Mining Fortune: +15; Grants +20% Mithril Powder.; Fuel Tank: Not Installed Increases fuel capacity with part installed.; Drill Engine: Not Installed Increases  Mining Speed with part installed.; Upgrade Module: Not Installed Applies a passive upgrade with part installed.; Apply Drill Parts to this Drill by talking to a Drill Mechanic!; Fuel: 3,000\ 3k; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 2.; RARE DRILL
The Mithril Drill SX-R226 is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]].
## Obtaining
The Mithril Drill SX-R226 can be forged in [[The Forge]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It takes 4 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mithril Drill SX-R226]] 1 Mithril Drill SX-R226
  - 3 [[Refined Mithril]] 480 (7 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 76,800 (1,200 stacks) [[Mithril]]
  - 1 [[Fuel Canister]] 2 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Coal]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Coal]]
  - 1 [[Drill Motor]] 10 [[Treasurite]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] 3 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 480 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 76,800 [[Redstone Dust]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
Drill:
Mithril Drill SX-R226 [[Damage]]: +65 [[Breaking Power]]: +5 [[Mining Speed]]: +450 [[Mining Fortune]]: +15
[Effects]
[Grants +20% ᠅ Mithril Powder.]
The Mithril Drill SX-R226 has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Mithril Drill SX-R226's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
This drill also gives a 20% Extra ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] which makes it good for grinding Mithril Powder.
### Forging
The Mithril Drill SX-R226, along with a [[Golden Plate]] and a [[Mithril Plate]], can be used to forge a [[Mithril Drill SX-R326]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- 1 [[Mithril Drill SX-R326]]
  - [[Mithril Drill SX-R226]] 1 Mithril Drill SX-R226 3 [[Refined Mithril]] 480 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 76,800 [[Mithril]] 1 [[Fuel Canister]] 2 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Coal]] 51,200 [[Coal]] 1 [[Drill Motor]] 10 [[Treasurite]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 [[Iron Ingot]] 3 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 480 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 76,800 [[Redstone Dust]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 1 [[Mithril Plate]] 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Titanium]] 5 [[Refined Mithril]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Mithril]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 [[Iron Ingot]]

# Mithril Plate
Categories: Rare items, Items, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
[Mithril Plate]
Type: Item

Item ID: MITHRIL_PLATE

The Mithril Plate is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
The Mithril Plate can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It requires 5 [[Refined Mithril]], 1 [[Golden Plate]], 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]], 1 [[Refined Titanium]] and 18 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mithril Plate]] 1 Mithril Plate
  - 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 5 [[Refined Mithril]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Mithril]]
  - 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]]
### Frozen Corpses
Mithril Plates can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Mithril Plate]] 1 Mithril Plate|0.15% (1 in 654)|
|Vanguard|[[Mithril Plate]] 1 Mithril Plate|1.71% (1 in 58.58)|

# Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank
Aliases: Mithril Infused Fuel Tank
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools, Mining, Dwarven Mines, Forged items

HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) Requirement: Heart of the Mountain III (3) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])

Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256,000x (4,000 stacks (256,000 items total)) [[Diamond]] 256,000x [[Mithril]] 256,000x [[Coal]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MITHRIL_FUEL_TANK
Tooltip: Drill Part; 10,000 Max Fuel Capacity -2% Pickaxe Ability Cooldown; Put this item in the Fuel Tank slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 3.; RARE
Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases the fuel capacity of [[Drills]] to 10,000 and reduces the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 2%.
## Obtaining
The Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It takes 24 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank]] 1 Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank
  - 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 (25 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 (4,000 stacks) [[Diamond]]
  - 10 [[Refined Mithril]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 256,000 [[Mithril]]
  - 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 [[Coal]]
## Usage
The Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to set its fuel capacity to 10,000 and reduce the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 2%. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### Forging
It is also used in forging the [[Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]] and takes 30 seconds.
- 1 [[Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank]]
  - [[Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank]] 1 Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 [[Diamond]] 10 [[Refined Mithril]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 256,000 [[Mithril]] 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 [[Coal]]
  - 10 [[Refined Titanium]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 [[Diamond]]
  - 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 [[Coal]]

Tooltip: Drill Part; +75 Mining Speed +5 Mining Fortune; Put this item in the Drill Engine slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 3.; RARE
The Mithril-Plated Drill Engine is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases [[Drills]]' [[Mining Speed]] by +75 and [[Mining Fortune]] by +5.
## Obtaining
The Mithril-Plated Drill Engine can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It takes 24 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mithril-Plated Drill Engine]] 1 Mithril-Plated Drill Engine
  - 2 [[Drill Motor]] 20 [[Treasurite]] 2 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] 6 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 960 (15 stacks) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 153,600 (2,400 stacks) [[Redstone Dust]] 2 [[Golden Plate]] 2 [[Refined Diamond]] 4 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 640 (10 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Diamond]] 4 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 640 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 102,400 [[Gold Ingot]] 10 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 1 [[Mithril Plate]] 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Titanium]] 5 [[Refined Mithril]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Mithril]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]]
## Usage
The Mithril-Plated Drill Engine can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to increase its stats by +75 [[Mining Speed]] and +5 [[Mining Fortune]]. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### Forging
It is also used in forging the [[Titanium-Plated Drill Engine]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]] and takes 30 seconds.

Moby's Shears are [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Shears]] used to harvest [[Glowing Mushroom|Glowing Mushrooms]].
## Obtaining
Moby's Shears can be purchased from [[Moby]] for 5 [[Chum Bucket|Full Chum Buckets]] and 15 [[Glowing Mushroom|Glowing Mushrooms]].
## Usage
Breaking a [[Glowing Mushroom]] with Moby's Shears grants an additional 1 [[Glowing Mushroom]].
## Trivia
- [[Farming Fortune]] does not affect [[Glowing Mushroom|Glowing Mushrooms]], so this item only changes the amount [[Glowing Mushroom|Glowing Mushrooms]] that drop when breaking from 1 to 2.

# Moby-Duck
Aliases: Moby Duck, Moby-Duck: Collector's Edition
Categories: Rare items, Epic items, Items, Aging Items
[Moby-Duck]
Type: Item

[Item Properties]

Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MOBY_DUCK_COLLECTOR_EDITION
Tooltip: Consume to boost your ☯ Fishing Wisdom for 60m.; Keep this item in your inventory to increase the bonus up to +30☯ Fishing Wisdom, at which point the item evolves!; Current Bonus: +0☯ Fishing Wisdom; A series of tales following a fellow named Moby and his waterfowl best friend. Who would be so egregious as to thrust such a literary masterpiece into the sea?; RARE
Moby-Duck is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that grants [[Fishing Wisdom]]. After reaching 30 bonus [[Fishing Wisdom]], it evolves into a Moby-Duck: Collector's Edition.
## Obtaining
Moby-Duck can be obtained as an OUTSTANDING JUNK CATCH by fishing with a [[Junk Sinker]] applied to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
## Usage
Upon reaching 30 bonus [[Fishing Wisdom]], the Moby-Duck evolves into a Moby-Duck: Collector's Edition with the following message.
EVOLUTION! Your Moby-Duck has evolved!
A Moby-Duck gains 0.1 [[Fishing Wisdom]] every hour of the player being online, taking 300 hours to reach the maximum buff of 30 [[Fishing Wisdom]]. This time can be sped up 2x by placing Moby-Duck into [[Time Pocket]]. Furthermore, the [[Enchanted Clock]] and the Quiz Puzzle in the [[Catacombs]] increases the timer on all [[Time Pocket|Aging Items]] by 1 hour, which is doubled for items placed in the [[Time Pocket]].
Using a Moby-Duck: Collector's Edition grants 30 [[Fishing Wisdom]], as well a permanent +1 [[Fishing Wisdom]]. This can only be done once.
## Trivia
- Moby-Duck is a reference to the popular 1851 novel Moby-Dick.
  - Likewise, [[Captain Baha]] is a reference to a major character in Moby-Dick, Captain Ahab.
- Moby-Duck is the only [[Time Pocket|Aging Item]] whose permanent bonus is only granted once.
## History

|Mar 12, 2025|Added Moby-Duck.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Added Moby-Duck.|

Giant ones can even create stones like this one.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Strength; Cubic Bonus Take -2%  Damage from  Infernal mobs.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 20.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Molten Cube is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Cubic]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Strength]] and [[Health]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv523]|[[Magma Boss]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
## Usage
### Reforging
The Molten Cube can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Cubic]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]] and [[Health]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XX (20)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Cubic]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Cubic Bonus, which decreases damage taken from [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs by 2%.

[Item Metadata]

Tooltip: Defense: +75; Ability: Bone Shield A Bone Shield will surround you, nullifying damage you take but consuming a bone in the process.; Bones regenerate every 30 seconds.; This item can be reforged!; RARE HELMET
Monster Hunter Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor]] Set composed of [[Skeleton's Helmet]], [[Guardian Chestplate]], [[Creeper Pants]], and [[Spider's Boots]], providing a full set bonus of +25% [[Damage]] to monsters and -25% [[Damage]] taken from monsters.
Replacing [[Spider's Boots]] with [[Tarantula Boots]] upgrades the armor set to [[Monster Raider Armor]]. The upgraded set increases the full set bonus of the armor to +35% [[Damage]] to monsters and -35% [[Damage]] taken from monsters.
This is a "secret" set, as none of the individual items show that they are part of it beforehand. When the set is complete, each item still retains its individual item ability and adds the full set bonus.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Skeleton's Helmet]]|Bone VIII (8) ([[Bone]])|320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Enchanted Bone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Bone]] x64 ×5 → [[Skeleton's Helmet]]|
|[[Guardian Chestplate]]|Prismarine Crystals V (5) ([[Prismarine Crystals]])|256x [[Prismarine Shard]] 256x [[Prismarine Crystals]]|Recipe: [[Prismarine Shard]] x64 ×4 + [[Prismarine Crystals]] x64 ×4 → [[Guardian Chestplate]]|
|[[Creeper Pants]]|Gunpowder VIII (8) ([[Gunpowder]])|224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) [[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] x32 ×7 → [[Creeper Pants]]|
|[[Spider's Boots]]|String VIII (8) ([[String]])|256x [[Enchanted String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted String]] x64 ×4 → [[Spider's Boots]]|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MOONGLADE_JEWEL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Moonglade reforge when combined with an Axe.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Foraging Fortune ☯ Foraging Wisdom; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 12.; RARE REFORGE STONE
A Moonglade Jewel is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Moonglade]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Axes]], which [[Foraging Fortune]] and [[Foraging Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
The Moonglade Jewel can be purchased from [[Agatha]] for 50 [[Agatha's Coupon]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Moonglade Jewel can be combined with an [[Axes|Axe]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Moonglade]] reforge, to grant [[Foraging Fortune]] and [[Foraging Wisdom]], which scale with the Axe's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XII (12)]]. An Axe that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Moonglade]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

# Moonstone
Aliases: Lunar
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Craftable items, Moonflower Collection, Collections
[Moonstone]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])

[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 204,800x (3,200 stacks (204,800 items total)) [[Moonflower]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MOONSTONE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Lunar reforge when combined with Equipment.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Speed ☯ Farming Wisdom; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 11.; RARE REFORGE STONE
A Moonstone is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Lunar]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Moonflower|Moonflower VII (7)]]. It grants [[Speed]] and [[Farming Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Moonflower VII (7) ([[Moonflower]])|8x Fine Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 8x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: [[Compacted Moonflower]] ×8 + [[Gemstone]] x8 → Moonstone|
## Usage
### Reforging
Moonstone can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Lunar]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Wisdom]] and [[Speed]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XI (11)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Lunar]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|

- Applying this reforge to [[Sun's Grasp]] will change the item's name to "Eclipse's Grasp", as a reference to the Solar eclipse.

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Murkbat Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Rekindle]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Rekindle ([[Attributes]]); Killing a Bat has a +10%–100% chance to add 1 Bonus Score to your Dungeon Run Limit of +1 Score per run.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Aliases: Super Magical Mushroom Soup
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Mushroom Collection

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 3x [[Planks]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 160x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 160x Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 160x [[Glowstone Dust]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SUPER_MAGIC_MUSHROOM_SOUP
Tooltip: Consuming this soup on your private island or garden allows the player to fly for 200 minutes.; Leaving the island will pause the effect!; RARE
Mystical Mushroom Soup is a [[Rarity|RARE]] consumable unlocked in [[Mushroom|Mushroom VII (7)]] that allows players to fly on [[Private Island|Private Islands]].
## Usage
When consumed, Mystical Mushroom Soup gives the player 200 minutes of flight on private islands. It also makes them invulnerable to fall damage while on their private island, which is useful when building farms. This soup can be a cheaper flight substitute for players without a [[Booster Cookie]]. However, the flight duration is limited and one should be aware if it is about to run out and drink more preemptively, or else you will fall and take damage or possibly die. The flight duration can be extended by consuming additional soup.
Mystical Mushroom Soup's duration will pause when leaving the island, resuming when one returns.
Warning messages will trigger when there are 60, 30, 10, 5, 4, 3, 2, and 1 second(s) left and when the effect runs out.
## Obtaining
Mystical Mushroom Soup can be obtained through crafting.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mushroom VII (7) ([[Mushroom]])|1x [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] 1x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 1x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] 1x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] 1x [[Bowl]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] + [[Bowl]] → Mystical Mushroom Soup|
## Game Message
|Condition|Random Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|Hmm... tasty! You can now fly for 200 minutes.|
|Consuming|I feel like I can fly! You can now fly for 200 minutes.|
|Consuming|What was in that soup? You can now fly for 200 minutes.|
|Consuming when; already active|Hmm... tasty! Your flight has been extended for 200 extra minutes.|
|Consuming when; already active|I feel like I can fly! Your flight has been extended for 200 extra minutes.|
|Consuming when; already active|What was in that soup? Your flight has been extended for 200 extra minutes.|
|Effect about to expire|Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 10s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 5s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 4s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 3s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 2s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 1s Mushroom Soup Flight has expired!|
## Trivia
- This was announced by a former 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Dctr, on June 4th, 2020.
- Although it requires 160 times as many resources as [[Magical Mushroom Soup]], it lasts 100 times as long, making it a less efficient use of resources.
- It was previously known as "Super Magical Mushroom Soup".
- The max flight duration is about 596,523 hours, or 2,147,483,647 seconds, which is the 32-bit signed integer limit. This amount of soup would cost .
  - This record was discovered and reached by Cekofari. Upon reaching this limit, the flight duration timer disappears, flying gets disabled, and eating additional soup does not re-enable flight. The only known way to regain flight on the private island is to eat a booster cookie.
- The first record was held by Zylenox, who had gotten 520,000 hours of flight, which he explained would last until he died in real life.
  - The next record was held by MaybeMoshi who beat Zylenox's record with 521,001 hours.
  - This record was ultimately broken by Cekofari when he hit the limit of 596,523 hours.
- The Mystical Mushroom Soup used to have a different appearance and gave the Nausea effect when consumed.
  - The Nausea effect has been removed and Mystical Mushroom Soup was given a different appearance and not considered a Mushroom Soup from vanilla Minecraft.
  - The custom Mystical Mushroom Soup was added because the vanilla Mushroom Soup interfered with the mechanics of the soup and caused it to not stack when crafted.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 4, 2020|Added Super Magical Mushroom Soup.|
|Jun 4, 2020|Renamed item to Mystical Mushroom Soup.|
|Oct 13, 2021|Changed from an actual mushroom soup item to a custom head item, as well as changed texture from to .|
|Oct 13, 2021|Removed Nausea Effect.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Mystical Mushroom Soup to the [[Bazaar]].|

Tooltip: Drops rarely from Griffin Burrows during Diana's Mythological Ritual.; A splendid shard that was buried in the earth by someone a long, long time ago.; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
A Mythos Fragment is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that drops rarely from [[Griffin Burrows]] while using an [[Archaic Spade|Archaic]] or [[Deific Spade]].
## Obtaining
A Mythos Fragment has a 6.76% chance to be excavated as a treasure from [[Griffin Burrows]] while using an [[Archaic Spade]] and a 9.31% chance from a [[Deific Spade]].

## Usage
### Reforging
Necromancer's Brooch can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Necrotic]] reforge, to grant [[Intelligence]], which scales with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XIX (19)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Necrotic]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+30 Int ([[Intelligence]])|+60 Int ([[Intelligence]])|+90 Int ([[Intelligence]])|+120 Int ([[Intelligence]])|+150 Int ([[Intelligence]])|+200 Int ([[Intelligence]])|+250 Int ([[Intelligence]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|

# Necron's Ladder
Aliases: Necrons Ladder, Necrons ladder
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Necron's Ladder]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NECRONS_LADDER
Tooltip: Consumable; While in your inventory, counts the number of Necron's Handles that you obtain from Dungeon Chests.; If you obtain 3 handles you can consume this item to permanently gain:; +1 Health +1 Intelligence +1 Magic Find; Handles obtained: 0\ 3; You may only gain this bonus once!; RARE
Necron's Ladder is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
Necron's Ladder can be purchased from the [[Regalia Room]] for 75,000,000 [[Coins]]. As it is a rotating item, it may not be available every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].
Necron's Ladder can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward during [[Raffle of the Century]] event.
## Usage
The player can keep Necron's Ladder in their inventory to track how many [[Necron's Handle|Necron's Handles]] they find in [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]].
If the player has multiple uncompleted Necron's Ladders in their inventory, only the one with the highest count of handles, or in case of a tie, the first one will be incremented.
Finding 3 [[Necron's Handle|Necron's Handles]] will allow the player to consume the item and gain a permanent stat bonus of the following:
- +1 [[Health]]
- +1 [[Magic Find]]
- +1 [[Intelligence]]
The player may only gain the bonus once.
## Game Message
|Condition|Guaranteed Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|You climb the 3 rungs of the ladder. It fails to hold your weight and shatters beneath you. You feel stronger than before. (You have unlocked the bonus for Necron's Ladder)|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 31, 2023|Added Necron's Ladder.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 2, 2023|Added Necron's Ladder.|
|Jan 30, 2025|Updated texture.|
|Feb 14, 2025|Added Necron's Ladder to the [[Regalia Room]].|

|Feb 14, 2025|Necron's Ladder now becomes consumable instead of resetting upon completion.|
|Feb 14, 2025|Fixed being able to increment multiple Necron's Ladders at once.|

The Neon Blue Sheep Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Neon Blue Sheep Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on January 5, 2021. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 500 purchases.
## Usage
The Neon Blue Sheep Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Sheep Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Nether Wart Basket
Aliases: Nether Wart Pouch
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools, Container
[Nether Wart Basket]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Anita]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NETHER_WART_POUCH
Tooltip: Ability: Nether Wart Storage LEFT CLICK Place nether wart in the pouch.; Ability: Alchemist's Bliss RIGHT CLICK Automatically plant nether wart on a row of soul sand.; RARE
A Nether Wart Basket is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that allows players to store or plant rows of [[Nether Wart]].
## Obtaining
A Nether Wart Basket can be bought from [[Anita]] for 2 [[Medals|Silver Medal]] and 30 [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]].
## Usage
Item:
Nether Wart Basket
[Effects]
[Ability: Nether Wart Storage LEFT CLICK; Place nether wart in the pouch. Ability: Alchemist's Bliss RIGHT CLICK; Automatically plant nether wart on a row of soul sand.]
- The storage size of the Nether Wart Storage ability is 54 slots or 3,456 items.
- Each use of Alchemist's Bliss sows a maximum of 250 (3 stacks plus 58) [[Nether Wart|Nether Warts]] on [[Soul Sand]], which are consumed upon use. If an obstacle appears on the row after using Alchemist's Bliss, the non-sown crops will not be refunded, even if that new obstacle is [[Nether Wart]].

Nether Wart Pouch Demonstration

# Noctilume
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Noctilume]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NOCTILUME
Tooltip: A delicate flower that needs just the right balance of day and night, changing colors to show when it's craving more light or darkness.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Noctilume is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Noctilume can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 6 [[Duskbloom]] and 6 [[Lonelily]] within 1 block of a 2x2 area of farmland.
While growing, a Noctilume needs water and has 4 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it spreads buffs and debuffs to adjacent crops, retains water by 100%, and decreases yield by -20%.
While growing, a Noctilume switches its flowers between a sun and moon. It can only advance to the next Growth Stage during the corresponding time.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Noctilumes also drop 1,590 (24 stacks plus 54) [[Cactus]], 2,120 (33 stacks plus 8) [[Wild Rose]], and +8,480 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Noctilume can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 300,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 150 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Noctilume grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Noctilumes are used to create a [[Stoplight Petal]] and [[Thunderling]].
## Gallery
- Noctilume in the Greenhouse when it craves darkness Noctilume in the [[Greenhouse]] when it craves darkness
- Noctilume in the Greenhouse when it craves daylight Noctilume in the [[Greenhouse]] when it craves daylight

# Not Very Moldy Bread
Aliases: Moldy Bread, Moldy bread, Very Moldy Bread
Categories: Rare items, Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Aging Items

Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NOT_VERY_MOLDY_BREAD
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VERY_MOLDY_BREAD
Tooltip: Consume to boost your ☯ Farming Wisdom for 60m.; Keep this item in your inventory to increase the bonus up to +30☯ Farming Wisdom, at which point the item evolves!; Current Bonus: +0☯ Farming Wisdom; RARE
Not Very Moldy Bread is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that grants [[Farming Wisdom]]. After reaching 30 bonus [[Farming Wisdom]], it evolves into Very Moldy Bread.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|16x [[Fine Flour]] 32x [[Cornucopia]]|Recipe: [[Cornucopia]] x4 ×8 + [[Fine Flour]] x16 → Not Very Moldy Bread|
## Usage
Upon reaching 30 bonus [[Farming Wisdom]], Not Very Moldy Bread evolves into Very Moldy Bread with the following message:
EVOLUTION! Your Not Very Moldy Bread has evolved!
Consuming Very Moldy Bread grants +30 [[Farming Wisdom]] for 60m, as well as a permanent +1 [[Farming Wisdom]]. The permanent buff can only be gained 5 times.
It gains +0.1 [[Farming Wisdom]] every 1h, taking 300h to reach the maximum buff of +30 bonus [[Farming Wisdom]]. This time can be sped up 2x by placing Not Very Moldy Bread into [[Time Pocket]]. Furthermore, the [[Enchanted Clock]] and the Quiz Puzzle in the [[Catacombs]] increases the timer on all [[Time Pocket|Aging Items]] by 1 hour, which is doubled for items placed in the [[Time Pocket]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming Not Very Moldy Bread|You consumed a Not Very Moldy Bread and gained +X☯ Farming Wisdom for 60m!|
|First Very Moldy Bread|It feels like eating a sponge with the aftertaste of some grass.|
|Second Very Moldy Bread|The mold coming into contact with your throat is a visceral, horrifying, feeling.|
|Third Very Moldy Bread|On the way down the mold scratches your tongue, the taste of pure moss shakes you to your core.|
|Fourth Very Moldy Bread|Your stomach cannot take much more of this.|
|Fifth Very Moldy Bread|As fast as it went down it started coming back up. You strained your throat, forcing it back down. Never again.|
## History

Obsidian Tablet is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Mythical]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Speed]], [[Strength]], [[Intelligence]], [[Crit Chance]], and [[Crit Damage]]. It also provides +150 [[Health]] and +40 [[Strength]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Obsidian IX (9) ([[Obsidian]])|1x [[Summoning Eye]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Obsidian]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Obsidian]] x24 ×8 + [[Summoning Eye]] → Obsidian Tablet|
## Requirements
The Obsidian Tablet requires [[Combat|Combat XX (20)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Obsidian Tablets at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Mythical [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

# Optical Lens
Aliases: Precise
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Mob Drops
This vicious material can sometimes be found on the floor of The Catacombs.
[Optical Lens]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])

[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Precise
Applied To: [[Bows]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXII (22) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OPTICAL_LENS
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Precise reforge when combined with a Bow.; This vicious material can sometimes be found on the floor of The Catacombs.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage; Precise Bonus Deal +10%  Damage when hitting a mob's head.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 22.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Optical Lens is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Precise]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Bows]], which grants [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]].
## Obtaining
The Optical Lens has a 1.23% chance to be obtained from defeating [[The Watcher]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Optical Lens can be applied to [[Bows]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Precise]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]], which scale with the bow's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXII (22)]]. A bow that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Precise]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Precise Bonus, which grants +10% more damage when hitting a mob's head.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+3 Str ([[Strength]]) +8 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +5 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+7 Str ([[Strength]]) +9 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +10 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+12 Str ([[Strength]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +18 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+18 Str ([[Strength]]) +11 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +32 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +13 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+34 Str ([[Strength]]) +15 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +70 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+45 Str ([[Strength]]) +18 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +90 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Precise Bonus; Deal +10% Damage when hitting a mob's head.|Precise Bonus; Deal +10% Damage when hitting a mob's head.|Precise Bonus; Deal +10% Damage when hitting a mob's head.|Precise Bonus; Deal +10% Damage when hitting a mob's head.|Precise Bonus; Deal +10% Damage when hitting a mob's head.|Precise Bonus; Deal +10% Damage when hitting a mob's head.|Precise Bonus; Deal +10% Damage when hitting a mob's head.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- This Reforge has the same boosts as the [[Reforging|Unreal]] Reforge at all rarities except [[Rarity|DIVINE]].
- [[Reforging|Precise]] can be considered a direct upgrade from [[Reforging|Headstrong]], as it grants more stats and higher damage bonus on head shots.
- This item is potentially a reference to the Lens item that drops from Demon Eyes in the game Terraria.
- Despite being called a material in its tooltip, it's not used to craft anything.

The Pangolin Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Atomized Whispers]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Pangolin Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Pangolin]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Fusion
A Pangolin Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Pangolin Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Atomized Whispers]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Atomized Whispers ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% extra Whispers.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Pangolin Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Pangolin Shard has the following description:
- It's covered entirely in a set of armored scales, except for its soft underbelly, which it curls into a ball to protect.

Tooltip: Health: +10; Farming Fortune: +5; Piece Bonus: Salesperson Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain  Farming Fortune.; Piece Bonus: +0 Next Upgrade: +1 (0\ 1); Right-click to equip!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Garden Level 4.; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 10.; RARE NECKLACE
Peony Set is a combination of four pieces of Peony [[Equipment]] which are all obtained from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] in [[The Garden]].
## Usage
Equipment:
|Necklace|Cloak|
|-|-|
|[[Peony Necklace]]; [[Health]]: +10 [[Farming Fortune]]: +5 Piece Bonus: Salesperson; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|[[Peony Cloak]]; [[Health]]: +10 [[Farming Fortune]]: +5 Piece Bonus: Salesperson; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|
|Belt|Bracelet|
|[[Peony Belt]]; [[Health]]: +10 [[Farming Fortune]]: +5 Piece Bonus: Salesperson; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|[[Peony Bracelet]]; [[Health]]: +10 [[Farming Fortune]]: +5 Piece Bonus: Salesperson; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|
[Total]
[Peony Set Health: +40 Farming Fortune: +20]
### Bonus Farming Fortune
Pieces of this set give a bonus to the player's [[Farming Fortune]]. This bonus is granted independently for every piece a player wears. The Peony Set's Salesperson bonus grants the player additional [[Farming Fortune]] depending on the amount of [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]] they have served.
|Bonus|Visitors Served|
|-|-|
|1 [[Farming Fortune]]|1|
|2 [[Farming Fortune]]|5|
|3 [[Farming Fortune]]|10|
|4 [[Farming Fortune]]|20|
|5 [[Farming Fortune]]|50|
|6 [[Farming Fortune]]|75|
|7 [[Farming Fortune]]|100|
|8 [[Farming Fortune]]|150|
|9 [[Farming Fortune]]|250|
|10 [[Farming Fortune]]|500|
|11 [[Farming Fortune]]|750|
|12 [[Farming Fortune]]|1,000|
|13 [[Farming Fortune]]|1,500|
|14 [[Farming Fortune]]|2,000|
|15 [[Farming Fortune]]|2,500|
## Notes
- It is important to note that the [[Farming Fortune]] from this equipment's Piece Bonus doesn't show in the items' stats, which might give the false impression of it giving up to 15 less than it actually gives.
- Unlike other equipment with upgradeable stats, the Peony set's [[Farming Fortune]] bonus milestone scales with the player's total Visitor milestone rather than the visitors served while the equipment is worn.

[Item Metadata]

Tooltip: Drill Part; 100,000 Max Fuel Capacity -10% Pickaxe Ability Cooldown; Put this item in the Fuel Tank slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 8.; RARE
The Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases the fuel capacity of [[Drills]] to 100,000 and reduces the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 10%.
## Obtaining
The Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]]. It takes 30s to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank]] 1 Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank
  - 1 [[Gemstone Fuel Tank]] 1 [[Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank]] 1 [[Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank]] 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 (25 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 (4,000 stacks) [[Diamond]] 10 [[Refined Mithril]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 256,000 [[Mithril]] 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 [[Coal]] 10 [[Refined Titanium]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Titanium]] 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 [[Diamond]] 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 [[Coal]] 4 [[Precursor Apparatus]] 4 [[Control Switch]] 4 [[Electron Transmitter]] 4 [[FTX 3070]] 4 [[Robotron Reflector]] 4 [[Superlite Motor]] 4 [[Synthetic Heart]] 10 [[Gemstone Mixture]] 40 [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]] 3,200 (50 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Jade Gemstone]] 256,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Jade Gemstone]] 40 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]] 3,200 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amber Gemstone]] 256,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Amber Gemstone]] 40 [[Gemstone|Fine Amethyst Gemstone]] 3,200 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amethyst Gemstone]] 256,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Amethyst Gemstone]] 40 [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]] 3,200 [[Gemstone|Flawed Sapphire Gemstone]] 256,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Sapphire Gemstone]] 3,200 [[Sludge Juice]]
  - 16 [[Precursor Apparatus]] 16 [[Control Switch]] 16 [[Electron Transmitter]] 16 [[FTX 3070]] 16 [[Robotron Reflector]] 16 [[Superlite Motor]] 16 [[Synthetic Heart]]
  - 25 [[Gemstone Mixture]] 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]] 8,000 (125 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Jade Gemstone]] 640,000 (10,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Jade Gemstone]] 100 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]] 8,000 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amber Gemstone]] 640,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Amber Gemstone]] 100 [[Gemstone|Fine Amethyst Gemstone]] 8,000 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amethyst Gemstone]] 640,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Amethyst Gemstone]] 100 [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]] 8,000 [[Gemstone|Flawed Sapphire Gemstone]] 640,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Sapphire Gemstone]] 8,000 [[Sludge Juice]]
  - 32 [[Plasma]]
## Usage
The Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to set its fuel capacity to 100,000 and reduce the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 10%. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_COMPACTOR_5000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Automatically turns certain materials in your inventory into their enchanted form.; Right-click to configure!; Does not grant Carpentry XP!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
Personal Compactor 5000 is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust VII (7)]]. Its ability turns certain materials in the inventory into their enchanted form similar to the [[Super Compactor 3000]]. This saves time crafting when grinding large quantities of items.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Redstone Dust VII (7) ([[Redstone Dust]])|1x [[Personal Compactor 4000]] 84x (1 stack plus 20 (84 in total)) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x12 ×7 + [[Personal Compactor 4000]] → Personal Compactor 5000|
## Usage
Right clicking the accessory while in the hand will open its configuration menu, where the player can place up to three different enchanted items in the Auto-Crafting slots. When there are enough items in the player's inventory, it will automatically turn them into the Enchanted form. It has 3 Slots.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Personal Compactor 6000]]|Redstone Dust VIII (8) ([[Redstone Dust]])|[[Personal Compactor 5000]] 1x Personal Compactor 5000 7x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] ×7 + Personal Compactor 5000 → [[Personal Compactor 6000]]|
## Trivia
- [[Experience Artifact]], Personal Compactor, [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]], [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.

|Unknown Date|No longer gives [[Carpentry]] xp.|
|Sep 21, 2021|Items placed in the Personal Compactor 5000 are no longer placed inside, but a duplicate, unusable item is placed inside.|
|Aug 25, 2023|Fixed adding the same items to Personal Compactor/Deletor.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed Crafting Recipe from 14 Enchanted Redstone Blocks ([[Enchanted Redstone Block]]) and 1 [[Personal Compactor 4000]] to 84 (1 stack plus 20) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] and 1 [[Personal Compactor 4000]].|

# Personal Deletor 5000

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 54,240x (847 stacks plus 32 (54,240 in total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Coal]] 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) [[Redstone Dust]]
Raw Materials to upgrade: 35,840x (560 stacks (35,840 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_DELETOR_5000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Automatically delete specific items on pickup.; Right-click to configure!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Personal Deletor 5000 is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingot X (10)]]. Its ability automatically deletes certain materials in the inventory when you pick them up. It is the upgrade of the [[Personal Deletor 4000]], and upgrades to the [[Personal Deletor 6000]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot X (10) ([[Iron Ingot]])|1x [[Personal Deletor 4000]] 224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x32 ×7 + [[Personal Deletor 4000]] → Personal Deletor 5000|
## Usage
Right-clicking the accessory while in the hand will open its configuration menu, where the player can place three items in the Auto-Deleting slot, similar to the [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactors]]. When the respective item enters the player's inventory, it will get deleted. It has 3 slots and also has a switch that must be turned on in order for the device to function.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Personal Deletor 6000]]|Iron Ingot XI (11) ([[Iron Ingot]])|[[Personal Deletor 5000]] 1x Personal Deletor 5000 448x (7 stacks (448 items total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x64 ×7 + Personal Deletor 5000 → [[Personal Deletor 6000]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]] will only remove items from your inventory that you pick up off the ground. This is regardless of the [[Rarity]] of the item. However, it will not delete any item that does not have the same SkyBlock ID of the item in the deletor.
  - Example: You obtain a personal deletor and place a [[Golden Sword]] inside, any item you pick up with the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD will be deleted. The [[Midas' Sword]], which uses the Golden Sword item for its texture, will not be deleted upon pickup, as it does not have the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD.
- Unlike mentioned previously, when the Personal Deletors were first released, if players placed a vanilla iron sword in the GUI, it would also cause every in-game item with an iron sword texture (e.g. the [[Giant's Sword]] and [[Hyperion]]) to be deleted. This led to the Deletor being temporarily disabled.
  - However, certain items that share the same SkyBlock ID will still get deleted. For example, since all potions have a SkyBlock ID of POTION, putting any potion in a personal deletor would result in all potions being deleted.
    - This was partially patched in the 0.19.10 update by preventing potions from being placed in Personal Deletors.
- Secrets could be deleted without receiving credit for finding them, by putting a Secret item like a [[Decoy]] in a Personal Deletor and attempting to pick up the item.
  - This was later patched.
- [[Experience Artifact]], [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactor]], Personal Deletor, [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.
## History

|Aug 25, 2023|Fixed adding the same items to Personal Compactor/Deletor.|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Potions can no longer be placed in Personal Deletors.|

# Pest Repellent MAX
Aliases: Pest Repellant MAX, Pest Repellant Max, Pest Repellent Max, Pest Spray Max

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PEST_REPELLENT_MAX
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume to cover yourself in a pleasant aroma that  Pests find truly revolting for 60m.; While active,  Pests will take 4x as long to spawn.; Right-click to consume!; RARE
Pest Repellent MAX is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
Pest Repellent MAX can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 1 [[Pest Repellent]] and 25 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Pest Repellent MAX can be consumed to reduce [[Pests|Pest]] spawn rates by 4x for 60 minutes. It does not reduce [[Bonus Pest Chance]].
Upon consuming, it gives the player the Pest Repellent II potion effect.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|YUM! Pests will now spawn 4x less while you break crops for the next 60m!|
|Attempting to consume when Pest Repellent II is already active|You already have this effect active! Try again in #m #s!|
## Tips
- Since you cannot consume another Pest Repellent while one is already active, it is recommended to consume one at the beginning of each hour if you plan to participate in Jacob's Contest. If you consume one right before or during a contest, you will likely have to stop for a few seconds in the middle of the next contest to consume another one when it expires.
  - You can consume a Pest Repellent MAX while a regular [[Pest Repellent]] is already active and the timer will reset to 60 minutes. However, if a Pest Repellent MAX is active, you cannot consume either one again until it expires.
  - You can drink a [[Milk Bucket]] to clear the effect early and be able to consume another one, however this will also clear all other active potion effects, including the [[God Potion]].

[Pest Trap]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PEST_TRAP
Tooltip: Place in a Garden plot and fill with bait to catch  Pests while offline.; Maximum Capacity: 2 Pests Limit of 3 Traps; RARE
A Pest Trap is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Pest Trap can be purchased from [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for 50 [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]].
## Usage
Pest Traps can be placed in [[The Garden|Garden Plots]] to occasionally catch [[Pests]]. Up to 3 Pest Traps can be placed at once. Pest baits can be placed inside to increase the chance of certain [[Pests]] spawning. Every [[Pests|Pest]] that spawns in a trap consumes one Pest Bait.
- [[Compost]] attracts [[Mosquito|Mosquitos]] and [[Earthworm|Earthworms]].
- [[Plant Matter]] attracts [[Locust|Locusts]] and [[Slug|Slugs]].
- [[Dung]] attracts [[Beetle|Beetles]] and [[Fly|Flies]].
- [[Honey Jar|Honey Jars]] attract [[Moth|Moths]] and [[Cricket|Crickets]].
- [[Tasty Cheese]] attracts [[Rat|Rats]] and [[Mite|Mites]].
- [[Jelly]] attracts [[Dragonfly|Dragonflies]] (only during the day), [[Firefly|Fireflies]] (only during the night), and [[Praying Mantis|Praying Mantises]].
- [[Moondew]] guarantees catching [[Lunar Moth|Lunar Moths]].
## Gallery
