# Pesto Goblin Omelette
Aliases: Green Goblin Omelette, Green Omelette
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools, Mining, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
[Pesto Goblin Omelette]
Type: Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: May cause nausea
HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) Requirement: Heart of the Mountain V (5) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Goblin Omelette]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 20,840 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Green Goblin Egg]] 96x [[Goblin Egg]] 512,000x (8,000 stacks (512,000 items total)) Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_OMELETTE_PESTO
Tooltip: Drill Part; Grants a 75% chance to not consume Fuel when mining a block.; Put this item in the Upgrade Module slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; May cause nausea; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 5.; RARE
Pesto Goblin Omelette is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]] upgrade module that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It grants a 75% chance to not consume [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mining a block, an upgraded form of the [[Goblin Omelette]].
## Obtaining
The Pesto Goblin Omelette can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It requires 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Green Goblin Egg]], 1 [[Goblin Omelette]], 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Jade Gemstone]], and 18 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Pesto Goblin Omelette]] 1 Pesto Goblin Omelette
  - 96 [[Green Goblin Egg]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Jade Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Jade Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Jade Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
## Usage
The Pesto Goblin Omelette can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to grant it a 75% chance to not consume Fuel when mining. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.

|Sep 30, 2026|Fixed Drill ([[Drills]]) Fuel ([[Machine Fuel]]) consumption warnings being sent multiple times if equipped with a [[Goblin Omelette]] [[Pesto Goblin Omelette]] Pesto Goblin Omelette module.|

|Unknown Date|Removed extra line in lore.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PIGS_FOOT
Tooltip: Speed: +1; Accessory Power: +8; Like a Rabbit's Foot, but more useless.; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Pig's Foot is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] obtained by beating [[Guber]] in a race on [[The End]] island. It gives +1 [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
The Pig's Foot can be obtained by completing the [[End Race]] in 48 seconds or less.
Once obtained, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Trivia
- The Pig's Foot Talisman was added into SkyBlock when popular Minecraft YouTuber Technoblade won an event titled Minecraft Monday and was promised by hypixel to get his own accessory. He had a noticeable reaction to the accessory.
- Its description is: "Like a Rabbit Foot, but more useless." as the Accessory is mainly only good for [[Accessory Power]].
- It is possible to complete the race illegitimately by getting a [[Remnant of the Eye]] and dying after getting the first checkpoint.
- The Pig's Foot granting +1 [[Speed]] could be a reference to Technoblade's video about him being the fastest man on SkyBlock. In the video, the reaction from the stat bonus the [[Bat Talisman]] gave could be why it gives +1 [[Speed]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Pig's Foot.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Pig's Foot was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|

Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: PIRANHA
Tooltip: Moonglade Serendipity I Increases the odds of finding rare items in Tree Gifts at Moonglade Marsh by +2%.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R11)
The Piranha Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Moonglade Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
A Piranha Shard can be obtained from [[Fishing]] in the [[Moonglade Marsh]] with [[Wooden Bait]]. There is a 90.91% (1 in 1.1 catches) chance for [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] drops to be replaced with one.
### Huntraps
A Piranha Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Murkwater Shallows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Piranha Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Piranha Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Moonglade Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Moonglade Serendipity ([[Attributes]]); Increases the odds of finding rare items in [[Tree Gifts]] at [[Moonglade Marsh]] by +2%–20%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Piranha Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Piranha Shard has the following description:
- In open waters, Piranhas swim in tight schools, peacefully coexisting until threatened.

# Pitchin' Koi
Aliases: Pitchin, Pitchin'
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Mob Drops, Fishing
[Pitchin' Koi]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Pitchin'
Applied To: [[Fishing Rods]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XVIII (18) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 22,222 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PITCHIN_KOI
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Pitchin' reforge when combined with a Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Fishing Speed  Sea Creature Chance; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 18.; RARE REFORGE STONE
A Pitchin' Koi is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Pitchin']] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], which grants [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv215]|[[Lava Leech]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.4%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Pitchin' Koi can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Pitchin']] reforge, to grant [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scale with the Fishing Rod's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XVIII (18)]]. A Fishing Rod that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Pitchin']] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

# Plant Diagnostics Tool
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools
[Plant Diagnostics Tool]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PLANT_DIAGNOSTICS_TOOL
Tooltip: Ability: Analyze RIGHT CLICK View the detailed properties of a plant inside the Greenhouse.; Ability: Sanity Check LEFT CLICK Click a block in your Greenhouse to find out what Mutations can grow there.; RARE
A Plant Diagnostics Tool is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Plant Diagnostics Tool can be claimed from [[Sam]] in [[The Garden]] as part of the quest to unlock the [[Greenhouse]]. It can also be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 50 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Tool:
Plant Diagnostics Tool
[Effects]
[Ability: Analyze RIGHT CLICK; View the detailed properties of a plant inside the Greenhouse Ability: Sanity Check LEFT CLICK; Click a block in your Greenhouse to find out what Mutations can grow there.]
The Analyze ability can be used by right-clicking on a plant in the [[Greenhouse]] to view detailed information, including its Growth Stage, Decay Info, Water Level, Effects, and Growth Status.
The Sanity Check ability can be used by left-clicking on an empty planting slot in the [[Greenhouse]] to view what [[Mutations]] are able to grow there.

Collection ([[Collections]]): Wild Rose III (3) ([[Wild Rose]])

Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 10,240 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 5,120x (80 stacks (5,120 items total)) [[Wild Rose]] 1x [[Plumber's Sponge]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1x [[Plumber's Sponge]] (50 [[Coins]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PLOT_ERASER
Tooltip: Right-click while on a plot to instantly clean it, saving you the trouble of doing it yourself!; However, the Plot Eraser gets comsumed in the process.; RARE
A Plot Eraser is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wild Rose III (3) ([[Wild Rose]])|1x [[Plumber's Sponge]] 32x [[Enchanted Wild Rose]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Wild Rose]] x4 ×8 + [[Plumber's Sponge]] → Plot Eraser|
## Usage
A Plot Eraser can be used on an uncleared plot in [[The Garden]] to consume it and instantly clean the plot with the following message:
Your plot has been cleaned, and the Plot Eraser has been consumed.
Time saved, mess gone!
Using a Plot Eraser does not physically modify the plot.

# Pocket Iceberg
Aliases: Scraped
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
Woah, woah, too many drops!
[Pocket Iceberg]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Woah, woah, too many drops!
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Scraped
Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXV (25) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 5x [[Glacite Jewel]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POCKET_ICEBERG
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Scraped reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Woah, woah, too many drops!; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Breaking Power ☯ Mining Wisdom; Scraped Bonus If the block you are mining has the same  Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 25.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Pocket Iceberg is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Scraped]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Breaking Power]] and [[Mining Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
The Pocket Iceberg can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 5 [[Glacite Jewel]] and 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Pocket Iceberg]] 1 Pocket Iceberg
  - 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Pocket Iceberg can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Scraped]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Wisdom]], which scales with the Mining Tool's rarity and 1 [[Breaking Power]]. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Scraped]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Scraped Bonus, which grants 25 [[Mining Fortune]] when breaking a resource with [[Breaking Power]] requirement that equals tools's [[Breaking Power]].

|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|Reforge Bonus|Scraped Bonus; If the block you are mining has the same Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.|Scraped Bonus; If the block you are mining has the same Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.|Scraped Bonus; If the block you are mining has the same Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.|Scraped Bonus; If the block you are mining has the same Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.|Scraped Bonus; If the block you are mining has the same Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.|Scraped Bonus; If the block you are mining has the same Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.|Scraped Bonus; If the block you are mining has the same Breaking Power as this item, +25 Mining Fortune.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Bugs
- Pocket Iceberg can still be applied to tools even without [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]].
## Trivia
- This reforge is recommended for the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] to reach 9 [[Breaking Power]], in order to break blocks the Gauntlet usually can't break, on top of which it also grants [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Wisdom]].

# Polarvoid Book
Aliases: Polarvoid
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Living Metal Heart Collection
[Polarvoid Book]
Type: Book
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Living Metal Heart V (5) ([[Living Metal Heart]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 20x [[Living Metal Heart]] 3x [[Frosty Crux]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POLARVOID_BOOK
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; When applied to a drill, grants +10 Mining Speed.; This can be applied to an item up to 5 times!; If at least 1 book is applied, grants +5 Mining Fortune.; X Rift-Transferable X RARE
The Polarvoid Book is a [[Rarity|RARE]] Book Modifier. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Living Metal Heart V (5) ([[Living Metal Heart]])|3x [[Frosty Crux]] 20x [[Living Metal Heart]]|Recipe: [[Frosty Crux]] ×3 + [[Living Metal Heart]] x20 → Polarvoid Book|
## Usage
A Polarvoid Book can be applied to a [[Drills|Drill]] up to 5 times via an [[Anvil]] in order to grant it +10 [[Mining Speed]]. Applying more than 5 Polarvoid Books on an item will still consume the book, even though a bonus will not be added.
If at least 1 Polarvoid Book has been applied to a [[Drills|Drill]], it will grant it +5 [[Mining Fortune]].
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Polarvoid Books can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the [[Ender Chest]] next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the Polarvoid Book can be used as mentioned above.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PORTABLE_CAMPFIRE
Tooltip: Can be placed down in the Glacite Tunnels.; Interacting with a campfire belonging to you or another player will reduce your  Cold by 50.; Each campfire can only be interacted with once, and you can only place one campfire every 5; minutes.; Consumed on use!; RARE
Portable Campfire is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
Portable Campfire can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]]. It requires 1 [[Refined Umber]], 16 [[Match-Sticks]], and 30 minutes to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Portable Campfire]] 1 Portable Campfire
  - 1 [[Refined Umber]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Umber]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Umber]]
  - 16 [[Match-Sticks]]
## Usage
A Portable Campfire can be placed down in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. Only 1 may be placed at a time. After 5 minutes, the Portable Campfire will disappear and another can be placed.
Right-clicking a Portable Campfire will remove 50 [[Cold]]. This can be done once per Portable Campfire.

The Portable Washer can be used to remove all active [[Sprayonator]] effects whilst on [[The Garden]].

# Presumed Gallon of Red Paint
Aliases: Blood-Soaked, Presumed Gallon Of Red Paint

[Reforge/Power Stone]

Applied To: [[Cloaks]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXIV (24) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 5,120x (80 stacks (5,120 items total)) [[Hemovibe]] 5,120x [[Cobblestone]] 12,800x (200 stacks (12,800 items total)) [[Dark Oak Log]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FULL_JAW_FANGING_KIT
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Blood-Soaked reforge when combined with a Cloak.; Not sure if paint.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Defense  Vitality; Blood-Soaked Bonus Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 14.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Presumed Gallon of Red Paint is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Blood-Soaked]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Cloaks]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Hemovibe|Hemovibe VIII (8)]]. It grants [[Health]], [[Defense]] and [[Vitality]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Hemovibe VIII (8) ([[Hemovibe]])|32x [[Hemoglass]] 32x [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x16 ×2 + [[Hemoglass]] x16 ×2 + [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x16 ×5 → Presumed Gallon of Red Paint|
## Usage
### Reforging
Presumed Gallon of Red Paint can be combined with a [[Cloaks|Cloak]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Blood-Soaked]] reforge, to grant [[Health]], [[Defense]] and [[Vitality]] which scale with the Cloak's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]]. A Cloak that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Blood-Soaked]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, the Blood-Soaked Bonus from this reforge grants +1 level to the player's [[Life Steal]], [[Drain]], and [[Vampirism]] enchantments.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+1 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +6 HP ([[Health]]) +1 Def ([[Defense]])|+1 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +8 HP ([[Health]]) +2 Def ([[Defense]])|+2 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +8 HP ([[Health]]) +3 Def ([[Defense]])|+2 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +9 HP ([[Health]]) +4 Def ([[Defense]])|+3 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +10 HP ([[Health]]) +5 Def ([[Defense]])|+3 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +12 HP ([[Health]]) +6 Def ([[Defense]])|+4 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +14 HP ([[Health]]) +7 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Blood-Soaked Bonus; Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.|Blood-Soaked Bonus; Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.|Blood-Soaked Bonus; Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.|Blood-Soaked Bonus; Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.|Blood-Soaked Bonus; Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.|Blood-Soaked Bonus; Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.|Blood-Soaked Bonus; Adds +1 Level to your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism enchantments.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- This is a great reforge for survivability, although it doesn't grant any damage. Especially if put on the [[Dragonfade Cloak]] as it gets tripled for [[Enderman Slayer]].

# Prismapump
Aliases: Prisma Pump, Prisma pump, Pump
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools
[Prismapump]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: When used, pumps water in the direction the player was facing.
Obtained: [[Anita]]
Uses: Placing water efficiently.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Anita]]
Buy: Bronze Medal ([[Medals]]) 2 [[Jacob's Ticket]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PRISMAPUMP
Tooltip: Ability: Pipeline RIGHT CLICK When used, pumps water in the direction the player was facing.; RARE
The Prismapump is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that places water in the direction the player is facing.
## Obtaining
[[Anita]] sells 4 Prismapumps for one [[Medals|Bronze Medal]] and 2 [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]].
## Usage
When used on a solid block, the Prismapump places water in the direction the player is facing until it hits a block or reaches the limit of 159 blocks. In the opposite direction, the water only flows as far as it would from a regular water bucket.
Since the 0.18 update, the Prismapump activates directly instead of being placed as a block. It remains in the player's inventory after use.
The Prismapump can only be used on a solid block; using it on water does not work.
## Trivia
- If the player has SkyBlock Addons installed, they have to disable the 'Avoid Placing Enchanted Items' function in order to use the item.
- Before the 0.18 update, the Prismapump was placed as a block, and water had to be placed on its side or top to activate it. The Prismapump could then be broken and reused elsewhere.

Prismapump Demonstration

The Puck Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Forest Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Puck Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Puck]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Fusion
A Puck Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Puck Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Forest Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Forest Serendipity ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +0.1%–1% chance for [[Tree Gifts]] to contain double loot.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Puck Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

- Pucks are mischievous forest spirits. If you see one, it's probably serving as a distraction.

# Puddle Jumper Hook
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Lotus Collection, Fishing, Tools
[Puddle Jumper Hook]
Type: Rod Part ([[Rod Parts]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Lotus VIII (8) ([[Lotus]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Fishing XXV (25) ([[Fishing]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 12,800x (200 stacks (12,800 items total)) [[Lotus]] 5,120x (80 stacks (5,120 items total)) [[Raw Cod]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PUDDLE_JUMPER_HOOK
Tooltip: Increases the chance to catch a Puddle Jumper by 400%.; Talk to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou to apply this Hook to a Fishing Rod.; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 25.; ❣ Requires Lotus Collection VIII.; RARE ROD PART
The Puddle Jumper Hook is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] unlocked at [[Lotus|Lotus VIII (8)]]. When applied to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] by [[Roddy]], it increases the chance to catch the [[Puddle Jumper]] by 400%.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lotus VIII (8) ([[Lotus]])|2x [[Gold Lotus]] 32x [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Gold Lotus]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] x32 → Puddle Jumper Hook|
## Trivia
- Despite being located lower in the [[Lotus|Lotus Collection]] than [[Trophy Line]], Puddle Jumper Hook has higher [[Fishing]] requirement.

The Puppy Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Puppy Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on October 20, 2020. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Puppy Skin can be applied to a [[Mastiff Armor|Mastiff Crown]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

The Purple Elephant Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Purple Elephant Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 13, 2021. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Purple Elephant Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Elephant Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.
